We then synthesize the guiding principles and concrete mechanisms of manipulation robustness across perception, planning, control, policy learning, and hardware, illustrating each mechanism through representative works, including foundational and recent studies. In addition, we revisit existing metrics and evaluation methods for quantifying manipulation robustness. Finally, we distill broader lessons for designing robust manipulation systems and discuss open problems and future directions toward achieving human-level robustness in robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [182] [Communication-Aware Robot Execution for Cloud Inference under Spatially Heterogeneous Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31497)
*Fengkai Liu,Yuichi Ohsita,Masayuki Murata,Hideyuki Shimonishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向云-机器人在异构无线网络下的请求-响应窗口框架，通过在机器人执行当前云生成原语的过程中，智能选择下一个请求提交点，确保上行可靠传输与下行响应获取，从而提升任务成功率、降低请求失败率与请求次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云托管基础模型使机器人具备超越机载计算限制的语义推理能力，但在空间异构无线连接环境下，机器人执行云生成的原语会变得脆弱：当前原语决定何时需要下一个结果，而无线环境决定何处能成功提交请求并接收响应。现有策略优化延迟或传输质量，但未能解决在支持可靠上传的同时又能保留可行响应获取机会的提交位置选择问题。

Method: 提出请求-响应窗口概念，量化一个完整云周期所需时间（上行传输、云推理、下行检索及推理不确定性）。基于该窗口和可用通信图，框架将下一次请求点的选择视为当前原语执行过程中的运动决策：选定一个在通信质量上足以支持云请求提交、同时又不超出当前原语有限支撑范围的位置。该请求点被融入局部规划器，引导机器人先运动至请求点再提交请求，随后继续任务执行并保持足够连接以获取云结果。

Result: 在基于实测数据的室内无线场景实验中，所提方法在任务成功率上达到最优或并列最优，同时使用更少的请求次数并实现更低的请求失败率。

Conclusion: 通过将通信感知的请求点选择融入运动规划，能有效缓解云-机器人在异构无线环境下的执行脆弱性，实现更鲁棒和高效的云辅助机器人任务执行。

Abstract: Cloud-hosted foundation models enable robots to use semantic reasoning beyond onboard computational limits. In this setting, the robot executes a currently available primitive generated by the cloud, and continued task progress requires the next cloud result before this primitive is exhausted. This execution becomes fragile under spatially heterogeneous connectivity, because the current primitive determines when the next result is needed, whereas the wireless environment determines where the next request can be submitted and where the response can be retrieved. Strategies that reduce latency or improve individual transmissions can shorten this dependency, but they do not determine a submission location that supports reliable upload and leaves a feasible opportunity for response retrieval. To address this problem, we introduce the request--response window, which characterizes the time required for the next cloud cycle, including uplink transmission, cloud inference, downlink retrieval, and inference uncertainty. Building on this window and an available communication map, the proposed framework treats the next request point as a motion decision during ongoing primitive execution, selecting it to provide sufficient communication quality for cloud request submission while preserving progress within the finite support of the current primitive. The selected request point is incorporated into a local planner, which guides the robot toward the request point before submission and then continues task execution while maintaining sufficient connectivity for retrieving the next cloud result. Experiments in an indoor wireless scenario built from measurements show that the proposed method achieves the best or tied-best task success among the compared methods, while using fewer request attempts and producing lower request failure rates.

</details>


### [183] [Stabilization Learning: A Paradigm Transition Bridging Control Theory and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31562)
*Quan Quan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合控制理论与机器学习的跨学科范式——稳定化学习，以稳定性为核心目标，区别于证书学习和强化学习。文章构建了基于六元组的统一数学框架，并扩展出带约束的七元组和带目标的七元组模型，涵盖控制、观测和识别三类应用场景，通过11类问题的形式化重构展示了框架的广泛适用性，并指出了未来两大发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习范式（如强化学习追求最优性、证书学习追求形式化证明）在面对复杂高维非线性系统时，缺乏以稳定性为核心的统一理论框架。本文旨在弥合控制理论与机器学习之间的鸿沟，提出稳定化学习这一新范式，为复杂系统控制提供兼具理论严谨性与工程实用性的解决方案。

Method: 采用基于Lyapunov的分析与设计、深度特征提取、数据驱动反馈合成等方法；构建了六元组统一数学框架，并扩展为两类七元组模型（带约束屏障空间的七元组和带目标的跟踪问题七元组）；对状态空间、受控系统、度量、策略等关键要素进行了形式化分析；通过11类问题（包括多智能体协同跟踪、视觉伺服机器人位姿稳定、棋类游戏、Push-T任务等）的形式化重构验证框架的适用性。

Result: 成功构建了稳定化学习的统一数学框架，展示了该框架在控制、观测、识别三类典型场景中的应用能力，并通过11类跨领域问题的形式化重构，证明了框架在多个领域（包括机器人、博弈、多智能体系统等）的潜在适用性。

Conclusion: 未来稳定化学习将聚焦于两大方向：构建统一的问题框架（进一步提升框架的完备性和通用性）以及实现高效且鲁棒的学习（解决实际应用中的计算效率与鲁棒性问题），从而为复杂系统控制提供兼具理论严谨性与工程实用性的解决方案。

Abstract: Stabilization learning is an interdisciplinary paradigm that bridges control theory and machine learning. Its core idea is to enable systems to adjust their policies under perturbations or environmental changes through real-time feedback and adaptive mechanisms. It takes stability as its primary goal, distinguishing itself from certificate learning, which focuses on formal proofs, and reinforcement learning, which pursues optimality. It encompasses a range of methods, including Lyapunov-based analysis and design, deep feature extraction, and data-driven feedback synthesis, and is applicable to complex high-dimensional, nonlinear systems. This paper elaborates on the two major categories of stability in stabilization learning, as well as three typical application scenarios: control, observation, and recognition. It constructs a unified mathematical framework based on a six-tuple, and expands into two types of seven-tuple models: constrained learning with barrier spaces and tracking problems with targets. It also analyzes the roles, meanings, and implementation choices of key elements such as state space, controlled system, metrics, and policy. Through the formal reformulation of 11 types of problems, including multi-agent cooperative tracking, visual servo robot position stabilization, chess games, and Push-T tasks, this paper illustrates the potential applicability of the framework across multiple domains. Finally, it points out that future stabilization learning will focus on two major directions: constructing a unified problem framework and achieving efficient and robust learning, providing solutions for complex system control that combine theoretical rigor with engineering practicality.

</details>


### [184] [DynFly: Dynamic-Aware Continuous Trajectory Generation for UAV Vision-Language Navigation in Urban Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31654)
*Wen Jiang,Hanfang Liang,Li Wang,Kangyao Huang,Wang Xu,Wei Fan,Jinyuan Liu,Shaoyu Liu,Hongwei Duan,Bin Xu,Xiangyang Ji,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynFly提出了一种动态感知的连续轨迹生成框架，通过B样条控制点空间表示和Spline-DiT生成器，结合无人机导向的动态感知监督，弥合了高层导航意图与可执行无人机运动之间的鸿沟，在OpenUAV基准上显著提升了导航性能和轨迹质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视觉语言导航方法主要关注离散动作、局部目标或稀疏航点预测，缺乏对从导航意图到可执行无人机运动之间连续过渡的建模，导致生成轨迹的连续性、稳定性和可执行性受限。

Method: 1) 在B样条控制点空间中表示专家轨迹；2) 采用Spline-DiT生成器通过流匹配学习条件轨迹生成；3) 引入面向无人机的动态感知监督，包括位置、有限差分速度/加速度、航向一致性及局部目标对齐。

Result: 在OpenUAV的Test Unseen Full子集上，DynFly相比最强基线提升了4.69 NDTW、2.40 SDTW、2.14 SR和4.87 OSR，并将NE降低4.51米。

Conclusion: DynFly有效弥合了导航意图与无人机运动之间的接口鸿沟，同时可集成到现有UAV-VLN框架中，兼顾了轨迹质量与原始视觉语言推理能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal large models have significantly improved UAV vision-language navigation (UAV-VLN) by enhancing high-level perception and reasoning. However, existing methods mainly focus on predicting discrete actions, local targets, or sparse waypoints, while the continuous transition from navigation intent to executable UAV motion remains weakly modeled. This motion-interface gap limits the continuity, stability, and executability of generated UAV trajectories. To address this gap, we propose DynFly, a dynamic-aware continuous trajectory generation framework that bridges high-level navigation reasoning and executable UAV motion. DynFly bridges high-level navigation intent and continuous UAV motion through a lightweight trajectory generation layer. Specifically, it represents expert trajectories in B-spline control-point space and employs a Spline-DiT generator to learn conditional trajectory generation via flow matching. Furthermore, we introduce UAV-oriented dynamic-aware supervision over position, finite-difference velocity, finite-difference acceleration, heading consistency, and local target alignment, enabling the generated trajectories to better satisfy UAV motion characteristics. And our trajectory generation framework can also be integrated with an existing UAV-VLN framework while preserving its original visual-language reasoning pipeline. Extensive experiments on the OpenUAV UAV-VLN benchmark show that DynFly improves both navigation performance and trajectory quality. On the Test Unseen Full split, DynFly improves the strongest baseline by 4.69 NDTW, 2.40 SDTW, 2.14 SR points and 4.87 OSR points, while reducing NE by 4.51 m.

</details>


### [185] [HABIT: Human-Aware Behavior and Interaction Training Dataset for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31682)
*Jaehwi Song,Suchae Jeong,Byeongguk Jeon,Sungdong Kim,Minjoon Seo,Hyungmok Son,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HABIT是一个包含10K+ episodes、160+小时的机器人示范数据集，在人类在场环境中采集，涵盖 Collaborator（协作）、Coworker（共事）和 Supervisor（监督）三种人机交互角色。实验表明，基于该数据集训练的策略能产生人类感知行为（时空同步、避让、手势理解），而纯机器人数据无法实现，且能快速适应新人机交互任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模机器人示范数据集均在人类缺席场景下采集，导致训练出的策略虽能独立完成任务，但缺乏人类感知行为（human-aware behaviors），无法在与人共享的环境中自然交互。

Method: 构建HABIT数据集：包含60个任务、10K+ episodes、160+小时，按三种人机交互角色（Collaborator、Coworker、Supervisor）组织数据，在人类在场环境中采集示范数据，并用此训练机器人策略。

Result: 训练于HABIT的策略在Collaborator任务中展现出时空同步，在Coworker任务中学会避让，在Supervisor任务中实现手势理解；而纯机器人数据无法产生这些行为。此外，HABIT训练的策略能快速适应新人机交互任务。

Conclusion: 将人类在场作为数据集多样性的新维度，能够将机器人策略从孤立任务执行扩展到与人共享的真实环境，弥合了现有示范数据集在人类感知行为方面的缺失。

Abstract: Large-scale demonstration datasets have been central to recent progress in general-purpose robot policies. However, existing datasets are collected in human-absent settings, and policies trained on such data may perform tasks competently in isolation but fail to exhibit human-aware behaviors. To address this gap, we introduce HABIT, a large-scale robot demonstration dataset for human-present environments. We organize tasks into three roles capturing distinct modes of human-robot interaction: Collaborator, where human and robot jointly accomplish a task; Coworker, where they pursue separate tasks in a shared space; and Supervisor, where the human directs the robot. The dataset comprises over 10K episodes and over 160 hours across 60 tasks. Our experiments show that training on human-present data elicits human-aware behaviors that robot-only data fails to produce: spatiotemporal synchronization in Collaborator tasks, yielding in Coworker tasks, and gesture grounding in Supervisor tasks. Moreover, training on HABIT enables rapid adaptation to new human-robot interaction tasks. By introducing human presence as a new axis of dataset diversity, HABIT extends robot policies to environments shared with humans.

</details>


### [186] [FastDSAC: Enhancing Policy Plasticity via Constrained Exploration for Scalable Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31691)
*Guanchen Lu,Yajuan Dun,Yi Zhou,Letian Tao,Jingliang Duan,Jie Li,Guofa Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastDSAC 提出了一种基于截断高斯分布的动作约束方法，通过隐式正则化缓解高吞吐量强化学习中的策略网络塑性损失，在并行采样场景下实现了更稳定、更高效的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高吞吐量采样架构虽然加速了离线强化学习训练，但数据量和更新频率的急剧增加破坏了基于价值方法的稳定性，并削弱了策略网络的塑性。

Method: 1) 引入截断高斯分布近似学习策略，排除分布外动作，保持必要随机性；2) 动作约束作为隐式正则化，对抗激进梯度更新导致的塑性损失；3) 采用连续高斯表示配自适应方差调节，替代离散价值分布，提升价值估计精度。

Result: 在MuJoCo Playground和HumanoidBench上的实验表明，FastDSAC不仅稳定了训练过程，在渐近性能和收敛速度上均优于当前最优基线。

Conclusion: FastDSAC通过隐式正则化机制有效解决了高更新-数据比场景下的塑性损失问题，为快速高效的并行强化学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Scalable reinforcement learning has popularized high-throughput sampling architectures, which significantly compresses the training time for off-policy methods in robotic locomotion. However, the rapid increase of data volume and update frequency undermines the stability of value-based methods and diminishes the plasticity of policy networks. To address these challenges, this work presents FastDSAC, a fast and high-performance variant of the Distributional Actor-Critic algorithm designed for parallel sampling scenarios. Specifically, we introduce a truncated Gaussian distribution to approximate the learned policy, which effectively excludes out-of-distribution actions that strain target value estimation while keeping necessary stochasticity for exploration. The proposed action constraint functions as an implicit regularization, which counteracts the plasticity loss typically caused by aggressive gradient updates. This preservation of network adaptability enhances sample efficiency, particularly in scenarios with a high update-to-data ratio, and accelerates the early training process. In contrast to prior fast reinforcement learning approaches that rely on discrete value distributions, our method utilizes a continuous Gaussian representation equipped with adaptive variance regulation, which improves value estimation accuracy by sampling confident and informative transitions. Extensive experiments on MuJoCo Playground and HumanoidBench demonstrate that FastDSAC not only stabilizes the overall training process but also achieves superior asymptotic performance and faster convergence compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [187] [RCT: A Robot-Collected Touch-Vision-Language Dataset for Tactile Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31694)
*Jingbo He,Michael Färber,Roberto Calandra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RCT是一个机器人大规模触觉-视觉-语言数据集，包含122种工业材料上的29,279个触觉帧。研究发现现有数据划分方式存在严重泄露问题（接触序列重叠导致评估虚高），当严格按材料留出评估时，Recall@1仅为25.1%，揭示了触觉感知在未知材料泛化上的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作开放世界物体时，触觉表征必须能泛化到未见过的材料上，但现有数据集和评估方法存在数据泄露问题，无法正确衡量模型对新材料的泛化能力。

Method: 收集了122种工业材料的29,279个触觉帧，使用三个DIGIT传感器在多个接触位置记录完整按压过程。设计接触序列感知的评估协议，严格区分按材料留出和按接触序列留出的评估方式。

Result: 帧随机划分导致数据泄露（同一按压的近重复帧同时出现在训练和测试集）；消除接触序列重叠后Recall@1下降17.7个百分点；材料留出评估下Recall@1仅25.1%；TVL/HCT公开划分中98.3%的测试序列在训练集中有原始像素最近邻。

Conclusion: 新材料的泛化是机器人触觉感知的核心挑战，现有结果被数据泄露严重高估；RCT提供了可复现的接触序列感知评估基准，均匀采样按压可改善对比学习效果。

Abstract: For robots manipulating open-world objects, tactile representations must generalize to unseen materials. We introduce RCT (Robotic Contact Tactile), a robot-collected touch-vision-language dataset with 29,279 tactile frames from full robot presses on 122 industrial reference materials in 7 categories, recorded with three DIGIT sensors at multiple contact positions. RCT preserves each press as a contact sequence, enabling held-out evaluation across materials, categories, sensors, contact positions, and contact sequences. Frames from one press are strongly correlated: frame-random splits can place near-duplicate observations of the same physical interaction in both training and test. With the encoder held fixed, removing contact-sequence overlap reduces tactile-to-text Recall@1 by 17.7 percentage points. When materials are additionally held out at training time, performance drops sharply, leaving held-out-material Recall@1 at 25.1 +/- 6.1% averaged over three held-out draws. The public TVL/HCT split shows the same structure: every test contact sequence appears in training, and raw-pixel nearest neighbors recover the correct sequence in 98.3% of cases. Uniformly sampling a press improves contrastive training, and RCT-trained embeddings improve category probes on unseen materials. RCT makes contact-sequence-aware, held-out-material evaluation reproducible and exposes novel-material generalization as a central challenge for robotic tactile perception. The RCT dataset is open-sourced at https://faerber-lab.github.io/RCT/

</details>


### [188] [UniTacVLA: Unified Tactile Understanding and Prediction in Vision Language Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31723)
*Xidong Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Jiaxin Shi,Fucai Zhu,Siyu Zhu,Michael Yu Wang,Xiaojun Wu,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种统一的触觉学习框架，将触觉信号建模为动态交互线索，通过触觉思维链推理和粗到细的未来触觉预测，构建状态与动力学感知的触觉先验，并结合触觉-动作混合控制器实现高频修正，在多种接触丰富操作任务中显著提升了成功率、操作精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在接触丰富的灵巧操作中表现受限，而VTLA方法将触觉信号仅作为被动辅助输入，难以建模触觉语义和未来物理交互。为此，作者提出将触觉信号作为动态交互线索，以实现接触理解与预测的统一框架。

Method: 1) 构建统一的触觉潜在空间；2) 通过触觉思维链推理和粗到细的未来触觉预测，联合建模当前触觉状态与未来接触变化，形成状态感知与动力学感知的触觉先验；3) 引入触觉-动作混合控制器，结合实时与预测的触觉反馈，对低频动作块进行高频修正。

Result: 在调整、插入、擦拭、装配四类接触丰富任务上，在干净和受外力扰动两种条件下，方法在成功率、操作精度和接触鲁棒性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一触觉学习框架通过主动建模触觉动态交互，有效提升了灵巧操作的性能，验证了其在物理交互中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved strong performance in many robotic manipulation tasks, yet remain limited in contact-rich dexterous manipulation. To overcome this limitation, recent vision-tactile-language-action (VTLA) methods incorporate tactile sensing into VLA models to provide direct contact information. However, they typically treat tactile signals as passive auxiliary inputs, making it difficult to model tactile semantics and future physical interactions. To this end, we propose a unified tactile learning framework for contact-rich manipulation that models tactile signals as dynamic interaction cues for both contact understanding and prediction. Specifically, we construct a unified tactile latent space and jointly model current tactile states and future contact changes through tactile chain-of-thought reasoning and coarse-to-fine future tactile prediction, thereby forming a state-aware and dynamics-aware tactile prior. Based on this prior, we introduce a tactile-action mixed controller that combines real-time and predicted tactile feedback to refine low-frequency action chunks with high-frequency corrections. Real-world experiments on four categories of contact-rich tasks, including adjustment, insertion, wiping, and assembly, under both clean and externally perturbed settings, show that our method improves success rate, manipulation accuracy, and contact robustness over existing methods, demonstrating its effectiveness in dexterous physical interaction.

</details>


### [189] [Autonomous UAV Navigation for Individual Wildlife Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31772)
*Claire Sun,Tanya Berger-Wolf,Jenna Kline*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自主无人机导航系统，该系统结合YOLOv11目标检测和DINOv2姿态分类器，实时优化图像采集以服务于下游的野生动物个体重识别（re-ID），通过主动朝向动物侧身并逼近至满足最小边界框阈值，实现了比被动航拍更高效的数据采集，并在斑马、长颈鹿、老虎、大象等物种上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有野生动物个体重识别依赖人工或公民科学家进行大量数据采集，劳动密集且效率低；而现有无人机系统多面向群体行为视频采集，未针对个体识别所需的图像质量（如侧身纹理、分辨率）进行优化，因此需要一种任务感知的自主导航系统，使下游re-ID需求驱动实时感知与控制。

Method: 构建自主无人机导航系统：①YOLOv11用于实时动物检测；②DINOv2-based姿态分类器判断是否露出目标侧身表面（即花纹识别所需的关键区域）；③系统根据检测与姿态信息实时决策飞行方向与距离，主动调整无人机位姿直至满足最小边界框阈值，从而采集高质量的re-ID图像。

Result: 在肯尼亚野外拍摄的斑马数据上验证了可行性，并证明该方法可推广至长颈鹿、老虎、大象等其他具有诊断性表面纹理的物种。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个任务感知的具身AI框架，使生态数据采集中的实时感知与控制由下游个体重识别需求驱动，有望显著降低野生动物监测的人力成本，提升大规模数据采集的自动化水平。

Abstract: Reliable individual re-identification (re-ID) of wildlife is essential for population monitoring, behavioral tracking, and conservation policy evaluation, yet large-scale data collection remains labor-intensive, relying on manual efforts by ecologists or citizen scientists. We propose an autonomous drone navigation system that actively optimizes image capture for downstream re-ID, moving beyond passive aerial sensing. The system combines YOLOv11 object detection with a DINOv2-based pose classifier to guide real-time flight decisions: detecting animals, orienting to expose the lateral flank (the surface of interest for pattern-based re-ID), and approaching until the subject meets a minimum bounding-box threshold. Unlike prior drone systems that optimize for group-level behavioral video, ours targets the specific image-quality requirements of individual-identification models. We demonstrate feasibility through a case study on zebra using footage collected in Kenya, and show the approach generalizes to other species with diagnostic surface patterns, including giraffes, tigers, and elephants. Our work establishes a framework for task-aware embodied AI for ecological data collection, in which downstream re-ID requirements drive real-time perception and control.

</details>


### [190] [Reinforcement Learning-Based Control for an Inline Skating Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31807)
*Ethan Marot,Thomas Bi,Clemens Schwarke,Victor Klemm,Marco Hutter,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过强化学习为被动轮滑鞋装备的人形机器人训练控制策略，使其实现动态、高效的轮滑 locomotion，无需依赖人类数据或模仿学习，在真实硬件上零样本迁移成功，能耗相比行走降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合人形机器人的灵活性与轮滑的高效、高速移动能力，解决传统人形机器人行走效率低、速度受限的问题，探索被动轮滑（而非主动驱动轮）在双足机器人上的动态控制可能性。

Method: 使用强化学习训练控制策略，配合球体和椭球体两种几何轮模型进行训练与验证以克服仿真接触伪影；设计定制成功导向指令课程和滚动奖励；策略直接零样本迁移到真实Booster T1人形机器人硬件。

Result: 相比标准行走步态，运输成本（CoT）降低高达50%；策略在真实硬件上成功实现动态平衡、抗主动物理扰动、高速转弯等敏捷运动能力。

Conclusion: 强化学习结合恰当的奖励设计和轮模型能使人形机器人学会高效的被动轮滑策略，且无需人类数据或先验知识即可零样本迁移到真实硬件，为人形机器人节能高速运动提供了新路径。

Abstract: As humanoid robots become increasingly dynamic, coupling them with reinforcement learning offers a promising approach to solving the complex, underactuated mechanics of passive inline skating. Equipping a humanoid robot with passive inline skating wheels presents an opportunity to combine the versatile agility of humanoids with the high-speed, energy-efficient locomotion strategies utilized by human skaters. In this paper, we train and deploy a reinforcement learning control policy that enables novel locomotion strategies for a humanoid robot modified to equip consumer inline skates instead of conventional feet. Unlike previous work limited to quadrupedal robots or actively driven wheels, our system allows for precise 6-DoF control of the skates to execute dynamic, edge-driven propulsion strategies. Our skating strategies emerge entirely from our reward structure, without reliance on human motion data, imitation learning, or kinematic priors. We overcome the inherent instability of passive wheels and simulation contact artifacts by utilizing different geometric wheel models (spherical and ellipsoidal) during training and validation, along with a custom success-based command curriculum and a specialized rolling reward. Consequently, our policy demonstrates up to a 50% reduction in Cost of Transport (CoT) compared to standard walking gaits. The resulting policy successfully transfers zero-shot to the physical Booster T1 hardware. Real-world deployments demonstrate dynamic balance, the ability to reject active physical perturbations, and agile locomotion strategies capable of turning at speed. A video of our results can be found at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-_APcOS7uFo.

</details>


### [191] [RoboTacDex: A Dexterous Visual-Tactile-Action Dataset for Humanoid Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31836)
*Xinyi Wang,Donghan Li,Zi'Ang Chen,Chong Yu,Chen Xin,Peng Ye,Yingkai Sun,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 RoboTacDex，一个基于 Unitree G1 人形机器人构建的大规模、多模态、多样化的灵巧操作数据集，包含 6k 条轨迹、19 个任务、23 种技能和 22 个物体，提供多视图 RGB、深度、触觉和语义标注，并评估了三种模仿学习模型，展示了数据集的有效性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习领域需要大规模、多样化的示范轨迹数据来提升机器人的操作能力，尤其是需要能模拟人类操作逻辑、涉及双臂和灵巧手的复杂任务数据集。

Method: 基于 Unitree G1 人形机器人构建数据集，包含 19 个任务、23 种技能、22 个物体共 6k 条轨迹；开发了改进的多相机同步系统实现毫秒级数据同步；记录多视角 RGB、深度、触觉反馈和语义标注等模态信息。

Result: 三种代表性模仿学习模型在不同任务类别上表现出各自优势与局限；成功试验结果和适度的泛化能力验证了数据集的多样性和有效性。

Conclusion: RoboTacDex 数据集因其大规模、多模态、多样化的特性，以及包含双臂灵巧手完成的高难度任务，为人形机器人灵巧操作学习提供了有价值的资源，数据集即将开源。

Abstract: In the field of robot learning, large-scale and diverse demonstration trajectories provide the fundamental basis for enhancing robotic manipulation ability. We introduce RoboTacDex, a large, multi-modal, and diverse dataset of dexterous manipulation behaviors performed with a humanoid robot. Built on the publicly accessible humanoid robot Unitree G1, RoboTacDex consists of 6k trajectories covering 19 tasks, 23 skills, and interactions with 22 objects. RoboTacDex provides comprehensive records including multi-view RGB and depth information, tactile feedback, and detailed semantic annotations. Furthermore, the dataset features a variety of relatively challenging tasks that can only be completed by dual arms and dexterous hands, aiming to mimic human-like operational logic and simulate real-world manipulation complexity. To ensure data collection quality, we develop an improved multi-camera synchronization system to enable millisecond data synchronization and recording of modalities. In our experiments, we evaluate three representative imitation learning models on our dataset,
  analyzing their performance as well as their respective strengths and limitations across different task categories. Successful trial results and a moderate level of generalization capabilities across a suite of tasks indicate the effectiveness and diversity of the collected dataset. Our dataset will be open-sourced soon.

</details>


### [192] [Bridging Local Observation and Global Simulation in Closed-Loop Traffic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31844)
*Ziyan Wang,Tan Xiang,Peng Chen,Xintao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRAFT提出了一种自监督的偏好对齐框架，通过故障发现和推理时重加权，解决自动驾驶模拟器因局部到全局上下文不匹配导致的异常行为问题，在无需重新训练基础模拟器的情况下，将碰撞率降低31.2%，交通违规减少33.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶模拟器在自车日志上训练时只学到局部上下文，但在全局可观测的闭环环境中部署时会产生上下文不匹配，导致异常停车、不安全交互等不真实行为。

Method: CRAFT包含三个核心步骤：1) 基于基础模拟器进行多样化的"假设"rollout，暴露上下文引起的故障；2) 用人类对齐的驾驶先验将这些故障转化为偏好监督信号，训练上下文偏好评估器（CPE）；3) 推理时CPE作为即插即用模块，在全场景上下文下对候选动作评分，重新加权自回归解码。

Result: 碰撞率降低31.2%，交通违规减少33.2%，且无需重新训练基础模拟器。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过自监督故障发现和偏好引导的测试时间对齐，有效缓解了局部到全局上下文偏差，是一种轻量级、可迁移的模拟器行为矫正方案。

Abstract: A local-to-global context mismatch arises when autoregressive traffic simulators trained on ego-centric driving logs are deployed in globally observable closed-loop environments. In such logs, the ego vehicle has rich local observations, while surrounding agents are only partially observed due to perception limits and occlusions. As a result, simulators may learn incomplete context--action mappings that remain hidden in log-based training but emerge during closed-loop rollouts, leading to unrealistic behaviors such as abnormal stops, unsafe interactions, and rule violations. We propose CRAFT, a Contextual pReference Alignment Framework for Traffic Simulation, to mitigate this mismatch via self-supervised failure discovery and preference-guided test-time alignment. CRAFT treats the base simulator as a globally observable sandbox, generating diverse what-if rollouts from logged initial states to expose context-induced failures. These failures are grounded with human-aligned driving priors and converted into preference supervision for training a Contextual Preference Evaluator (CPE). At inference time, CPE acts as a plug-in alignment module that scores candidate actions under complete scene context and reweights autoregressive decoding toward globally coherent behaviors. CRAFT mitigates this local-to-global contextual bias, reducing collisions by 31.2\% and traffic violations by 33.2\% without retraining the base simulator.

</details>


### [193] [Z-1: Efficient Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31846)
*Lang Cao,Renhong Chen,Luyi Li,Peng Wang,Mofan Peng,Yitong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Z-1是一个基于强化学习（GRPO）的后训练框架，用于流式VLA模型，在24个RoboCasa任务上将平均成功率提升至80.6%，相比SFT初始化提高13.2%，且无需额外私有数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型大多依赖行为克隆或监督微调（SFT），无法从自身失败中学习改进，限制了策略性能的提升空间。

Method: 基于π₀.₅，使用公开RoboCasa演示进行SFT，然后应用任务级别的Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）策略，结合共享前缀轨迹构建、树状轨迹分支、完成感知奖励校准以及VLM和Action Expert的选择性联合训练。

Result: 在全部24个RoboCasa任务上，平均成功率达80.6%，相比SFT初始化提升13.2个百分点，并超越已发表的最优模型。

Conclusion: 系统性的GRPO后训练可以在无需额外私有演示的情况下显著提升流式VLA策略的性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising framework for robotic manipulation by connecting language instructions, visual observations, and continuous control. However, most existing policies remain limited by behavior cloning or supervised fine-tuning (SFT) from fixed demonstrations, which provides limited opportunity to improve from the policy's own failures. In this paper, we present Z-1, a reinforcement learning (RL) post-training framework for flow-based VLA models. Built on top of $π_{0.5}$, Z-1 uses only publicly released RoboCasa demonstrations for SFT and then applies a task-wise Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) strategy across $24$ standard RoboCasa tasks. To improve the efficiency and stability of online optimization, Z-1 combines shared-prefix rollout construction, tree-structured trajectory branching, completion-aware reward calibration, and selective joint training of VLM and Action Expert. Across all $24$ RoboCasa tasks, Z-1 achieves an average success rate of $80.6\%$, improving over its SFT initialization by $13.2\%$ points and outperforms the published sota models. These results show that systematic GRPO post-training can substantially improve flow-based VLA policies without additional private demonstrations.

</details>


### [194] [Improving path-tracking performance of an articulated tractor-trailer system using a non-linear kinematic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31889)
*Marina Murillo,Guido Sanchez,Nestor Deniz,Lucas Genzelis,Leonardo Giovanini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种铰接式拖拉机-挂车系统的非线性数学模型，结合模型预测控制（MPC），在精确农业场景中实现了对挂车位置而非拖拉机位置的精确路径跟踪，显著降低了挂车在直线和地头转弯时的位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接式拖拉机-挂车系统在精确农业（如小地块播种、喷洒、收割）中具有重要应用价值，但传统控制方法难以有效跟踪挂车路径，导致作业精度不足。为此，需要一种能够直接控制挂车位置的控制模型。

Method: 建立铰接式拖拉机-挂车系统的非线性数学模型，将其嵌入非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC）中，实时监测挂车位置；在控制器中考虑挂车运动学，通过ROS和Gazebo仿真验证效果。

Result: 考虑挂车运动学后，挂车位置偏差在直线路径和地头转弯路径上均显著降低；实现了直接控制挂车位置而非拖拉机位置的控制目标。

Conclusion: 所提出的数学模型结合MPC能够有效提升铰接式拖拉机-挂车系统的路径跟踪精度，特别适用于精确农业中要求挂车精准循迹的作业场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel non-linear mathematical model of an articulated tractor-trailer system that can be used, in combination with receding horizon techniques, to improve the performance of path tracking tasks of articulated systems. Due to its dual steering mechanisms, this type of vehicle can be very useful in precision agriculture, particularly for seeding, spraying and harvesting in small fields. The articulated tractor-trailer system model was embedded within a non-linear model predictive controller and the trailer position was monitored. When the kinematic of the trailer was considered, the deviation of trailer's position was reduced substantially alongside not only straight paths but also in headland turns. Using the proposed mathematical model, we were able to control the trailer's position itself rather than the tractor's position. The Robot Operating System (ROS) framework and Gazebo simulator were used to perform realistic simulations examples.

</details>


### [195] [CoDex: Learning Compositional Dexterous Functional Manipulation without Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31909)
*Bowen Jiang,William Painter Reger,Roberto Martin-Martin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoDex是一个零演示框架，利用视觉-语言模型和解析优化+强化学习，使机器人能自主发现并执行组合性灵巧功能性物体操作（如喷壶喷水、胶枪涂胶等），无需人类示范即可从仿真迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决组合性灵巧功能性物体操作（CD-FOM）中语义理解与物理灵巧性难以融合的问题，现有方法依赖大量人类演示，缺乏自主发现操作策略的能力。

Method: CoDex框架：使用视觉-语言模型（VLMs）推断语义约束，引导解析约束优化生成功能性抓取候选，再通过强化学习（RL）精化完整"抓取-移动-驱动"策略，实现仿真到现实的迁移。

Result: 在7-DoF机械臂+16-DoF多指手上验证了6种CD-FOM任务（喷壶、热胶枪、气吹、手电筒、胡椒研磨器等），成功自主发现并执行复杂灵巧行为，无需人类演示。

Conclusion: CoDex证明了结合视觉-语言模型的语义理解与优化+强化学习的物理推理，可以在零演示条件下自主发现复杂的灵巧功能性操作策略，具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: In this work, we study Compositional Dexterous Functional Object Manipulation (CD-FOM): tasks such as aiming and actuating a spray bottle on a plant or a glue gun on wood, which require both actuating an object's internal mechanism and controlling its pose to apply the object's function to the environment. These tasks pose significant challenges for robots due to the demanding integration of semantic understanding of the object's function, actuation mode, and application area with intricate physical dexterity to manage grasp stability, movement trajectory, and actuation. We introduce CoDex, a zero-demonstration framework that autonomously discovers CD-FOM manipulation strategies. CoDex uses vision-language models (VLMs) to infer semantic constraints from the task and scene. These constraints guide analytic constrained optimization to generate a short list of functional grasp candidates that can be efficiently refined with reinforcement learning to generate full grasp-move-actuate policies transferable from simulation to the real world. We evaluate CoDex on a 7-DoF robot arm with a 16-DoF multi-fingered hand across six CD-FOM tasks involving previously unseen objects with internal mechanisms, including spray bottles, hot glue guns, air dusters, flashlights, and pepper grinders, and their application to unseen target objects, showcasing its ability to autonomously discover and execute complex, physically viable dexterous behaviors without human demonstrations. More information at https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/CoDex/.

</details>


### [196] [Learning Locomotion on Discrete Terrain via Minimal Proximity Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31912)
*Jiale Fan,Connor Flynn,Tianao Xu,Junzhe He,Andrei Cramariuc,Marco Hutter,Robert Baines*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出在四足机器人足部嵌入低成本、高频红外接近传感器，通过强化学习框架提供接触前感知，使机器人能够预判地形不连续（如缝隙、垫脚石），在离散地形上显著提升穿越鲁棒性，且计算需求远低于传统视觉方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全局感知（LiDAR、深度相机）存在延迟、遮挡和高计算成本的问题，而本体感觉仅能在接触后反应。因此需要一种低延迟、低计算、抗遮挡的预接触感知方案。

Method: 在四足机器人足部嵌入低成本、高频红外接近传感器，提供局部预接触反馈；将该传感器信号融入强化学习框架，在仿真中建模并迁移到真实世界。

Result: 实验表明，局部接近感知在离散地形（如缝隙、垫脚石）上显著提升了穿越鲁棒性，且仿真到现实迁移保真度高。

Conclusion: 这种稀疏的近场感知为复杂全局感知系统提供了低功耗、低延迟的替代或补充方案，特别适用于不可预测的环境中。

Abstract: Learning-based control has revolutionized dynamic locomotion, yet navigating unstructured terrain remains limited by a robot's incomplete awareness of imminent ground contact. While global perception systems such as LiDARs and depth cameras provide environmental context, they are frequently plagued by latencies, occlusions, and the high computational cost of dense geometric reconstruction. On the other hand, proprioceptive feedback is purely reactive, initiating corrections only after impact has occurred. This work explores embedding a minimal suite of low-cost, high-frequency infrared proximity sensors directly into the feet of a quadrupedal robot. These sensors provide "pre-contact" feedback that is robust to self-occlusions and significantly less computationally demanding than conventional vision-based pipelines. By integrating these localized signals into a reinforcement learning framework, we enable the robot to anticipate terrain discontinuities such as gaps and stepping stones that are problematic for traditional perception stacks due to occlusions or state estimation drift. We demonstrate that such sparse, near-field sensing can be reliably modeled in simulation and transferred to the real world with high fidelity. Experimental results show that local proximity sensing substantially improves traversal robustness over discrete terrain and offers a low-power, low-latency alternative or complement to complex global perception suites in unpredictable environments. For more information about results and methods, please see the project website: https://sites.google.com/view/foot-tof/home.

</details>


### [197] [MVP-Nav: Multi-layer Value Map Planner Navigator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31919)
*Wenyuan Xie,Shaokai Wu,Yijin Zhou,Yanbiao Ji,Guodong Zhang,Bayram Bayramli,Qiuchang Li,Xunchu Zhou,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVP-Nav提出了一种仅依赖RGB感知的物理感知导航框架，通过3D基础模型重建物理占据信息，并引入多层价值图统一语义推理与物理约束，在零样本目标导航任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RGB的零样本目标导航方法缺乏几何约束，导致语义合理但物理不安全的动作，亟需一种无需深度传感器但能对齐物理世界的导航框架。

Method: 1) 利用3D基础模型将2D语义实例投影为3D有向边界框，重建显式物理占据图；2) 提出多层价值图(MVM)，将语义优先级与重建几何融合到共享代价空间中，实现物理约束下的几何规划。

Result: 在零样本目标导航基准上显著超越现有深度无关方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 验证了结构化物理先验可以有效补偿主动深度传感器的缺失，为纯RGB物理感知导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot Object Goal Navigation (ZSON) with RGB-only perception poses a fundamental challenge for embodied agents, as the absence of explicit depth information introduces severe physical uncertainty and semantic-physical misalignment. Existing approaches either rely on high-level semantic reasoning without geometric grounding or learn end-to-end policies that lack explicit physical constraints, often resulting in semantically plausible but physically unsafe behaviors. In this paper, we propose MVP-Nav, a physical-aware RGB-only navigation framework that aligns perception, planning, and control with the real 3D world. MVP-Nav reconstructs explicit physical occupancy from monocular observations by leveraging 3D foundation models to project 2D semantic instances into 3D oriented bounding boxes, forming a global spatial semantic representation. To unify high-level semantic reasoning and low-level physical constraints, we introduce a Multi-layer Value Map (MVM) that integrates semantic priorities and reconstructed geometry into a shared cost space, enabling physically grounded geometric planning. Extensive experiments on zero-shot object navigation benchmarks demonstrate that MVP-Nav significantly outperforms existing depth-free methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance and validating that structured physical priors can effectively compensate for the absence of active depth sensors.

</details>


### [198] [LeCropFollow: Latent Space Planning for Navigation in Unstructured Crop Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31941)
*Felipe Tommaselli,Francisco Affonso,Arthur Pompeu,Gianluca Capezzuto,Arun Narenthiran Sivakumar,Girish Chowdhary,Marcelo Becker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LeCropFollow 提出一种隐式规划框架，绕过几何建模，利用自监督语义热图+MBRL（TD-MPC2）在潜空间优化轨迹，实现零样本从仿真到真实世界的迁移，在玉米田非结构化场景中语义失败率较关键点方法降低2.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何方法将高维视觉数据压缩为确定性空间参考，丢弃了导航歧义地形所需的不确定性和语义上下文，导致非结构化场景（不规则种植、间断）中频繁失败。

Method: 1) 自监督语义热图提取器；2) 结合TD-MPC2（基于模型的强化学习规划器）在潜流形上直接优化轨迹；3) 对未压缩热图信号进行操作，保留语义上下文。

Result: 在晚期玉米田实验中，LeCropFollow在非结构化行中与SOTA基线持平，在种植间断处语义失败率降低2.4倍，并实现零样本从仿真到真实世界的迁移。

Conclusion: 隐式规划为异构农业环境中的导航提供了一种鲁棒的几何估计替代方案。

Abstract: Unstructured navigational features, such as irregular planting or discontinuities, remain the primary failure mode for under-canopy agricultural robots. Existing geometric approaches often fail in these scenarios because they compress high-dimensional visual data into deterministic spatial references, effectively discarding the uncertainty and semantic context required to navigate ambiguous terrain. To address this, we present LeCropFollow, a visual navigation framework that bypasses explicit geometric modeling in favor of a learned latent representation. By integrating a self-supervised semantic heatmap extractor with TD-MPC2, a Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (MBRL) planner, our system optimizes trajectories directly within a latent manifold. The framework operates over the uncompressed heatmap signal, preserving the semantic context that geometric reductions discard. We demonstrate that this representational shift enables zero-shot transfer from simplified simulation to the physical world without fine-tuning. Extensive field experiments in late-stage corn fields show that LeCropFollow matches state-of-the-art baselines in unstructured rows but significantly outperforms them in plantation gaps, achieving a 2.4x reduction in semantic failures compared to keypoint-based methods. These results suggest that latent planning offers a robust alternative to geometric estimation for operations in heterogeneous agricultural environments. Code, models, and data available: https://felipe-tommaselli.github.io/lecropfollow .

</details>


### [199] [RRT-Rope: A deterministic shortening approach for fast near-optimal path planning in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31948)
*Louis Petit,Alexis Lussier Desbiens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RRT-Rope算法，通过改进的RRT-Connect快速生成可行路径，再利用确定性捷径技术优化路径，在大型3D环境（如地下采矿巷道）中比现有RRT变体快70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRT变体（如Informed RRT*, RRT#）在大型3D环境中收敛时间过长，而传统RRT的捷径化虽有效但仍有提升空间，亟需一种能够在极短时间内找到近优解的路径规划算法。

Method: 1. 使用改进版RRT-Connect快速计算可行路径；2. 利用树分支中添加的中间节点，采用确定性捷径技术对路径进行后处理优化。

Result: 在所有仿真环境中，RRT-Rope在路径代价和计算时间上均优于其他流行RRT变体和缩短技术；在代表性采矿巷道中，比次优算法快70%。

Conclusion: RRT-Rope通过在大型无遮挡3D环境中大幅缩短计算时间的同时保持近优路径质量，为无人机在采矿巷道等场景下的实时路径规划提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Many path planning algorithms have been introduced so far, but most are costly, in path cost and in processing time, in large-scale uncluttered 3D environments such as underground mining stopes explored by an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) algorithms are popular because of their probabilistic completeness and rapidity in finding a feasible path in single-query problems. Many of the algorithms (e.g. Informed RRT*, RRT#) developed to improve RRT need considerable time to converge in large environments. Shortcutting an RRT is an old idea that has been proven to outperform RRT variants. This paper introduces a new method, RRT-Rope, that aims at finding a near-optimal solution in a drastically shorter amount of time. The proposed approach benefits from fast computation of a feasible path with an altered version of RRT-connect, and post-processes it quickly with a deterministic shortcutting technique, taking advantage of intermediate nodes added to each branch of the tree. This paper presents simulations and statistics carried out to show the efficiency of RRT-Rope, which gives better results in terms of path cost and computation time than other popular RRT variations and shortening techniques in all our simulation environments, and is up to 70% faster than the next best algorithm in a representative stope.

</details>


### [200] [Adapting Generalist Robot Policies with Semantic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31958)
*Jagdeep Singh Bhatia,Andrew Wagenmaker,William Chen,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出语义动作强化学习（SARL），通过在线交互优化语言提示空间来利用通用策略中的已有技能，从而解决复杂、长视距任务，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略通过大规模预训练获得多样化行为库，但标准强化学习方法直接优化动作空间，要求策略的动作分布从一开始就接近高效策略，这对超出预训练分布的复杂或长视距任务不成立。

Method: 提出语义动作强化学习（SARL），将语言提示作为可优化的动作空间，通过在线交互学习调制语言输入，将通用策略视为可控技能先验，实现结构化、语义有意义的探索和高效的在线改进。

Result: 在真实世界和模拟基准测试中，SARL能够使VLA策略适应解决复杂、长视距任务，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 对于足够表达力的通用策略，语言提示是学习解决复杂任务的有效替代空间，利用已有技能组合而非从头学习新技能，能实现高效在线改进和鲁棒的任务求解。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies learn a diverse repertoire of behaviors from large-scale pretraining. In principle, this makes them excellent priors for downstream adaptation via reinforcement learning (RL). In practice, however, standard RL methods leveraging this prior optimize directly over robot actions, requiring the base policy's action distribution to be close to that of a performant policy from the start. This assumption breaks down for complex or long-horizon tasks that fall outside the pretraining distribution. Our key insight is that, for sufficiently expressive generalist policies, language prompts are an effective alternative space for learning to solve such tasks: modulating language inputs elicits skills already within the policy's repertoire, which can be composed to solve tasks beyond its zero-shot capabilities. We propose Semantic Action Reinforcement Learning (SARL), which learns to optimize this prompt space through online interaction, treating the generalist policy as a controllable skill prior. Importantly, leveraging pretrained skills rather than learning new ones from scratch yields structured, semantically meaningful exploration and highly efficient online improvement, and learning to modulate prompts through experience grounds them in induced real-world behaviors for robust task-solving. Across real-world settings and simulated benchmarks, we show SARL unlocks fundamentally new capabilities -- adapting VLA behavior to solve complex, long-horizon tasks -- and significantly outperforms existing approaches for improving robot behavior in deployment.

</details>


### [201] [OopsieVerse: A Safety Benchmark with Damage-Aware Simulation for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.31993)
*Arnav Balaji,Arpit Bahety,Sriniket Ambatipudi,Daniel Lam,Junhong Xu,Roberto Martín-Martín*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OOPSIEVERSE，一个统一的仿真框架和基准，用于检测、量化和表示机器人操作中的物理损伤（机械、热、液体），支持安全操作策略的学习与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真器缺乏通用机制来检测、量化和表示损伤，导致物理安全性成为家用机器人部署的主要障碍。

Method: 提出DAMAGESIM框架，将接触力、温度变化、液体交互等转化为机械、热或流体损伤信号；在OmniGibson和RoboCasa两个仿真器中实例化，并设计多类家用任务。

Result: 在四个用例中验证：实时损伤反馈引导安全数据收集、损伤条件化模仿/强化学习训练安全策略、评估VLA策略安全性、提升sim-to-real迁移的实际安全性。

Conclusion: OOPSIEVERSE可作为开源基础，支持系统性、可扩展的机器人安全操作研究。

Abstract: While robotic manipulation capabilities have advanced rapidly, physical safety remains a major barrier to deploying household robots: task success is insufficient if the robot damages itself or its surroundings. Simulation offers a harm-free alternative to costly and dangerous real-world training and evaluation, yet existing simulators lack general mechanisms to detect, quantify, and represent damage. To address this gap, we introduce OOPSIEVERSE, a unified simulation framework and benchmark for damage-aware household manipulation. OOPSIEVERSE provides damage as an explicit, physically-grounded, and taskagnostic signal by converting sources such as contact forces, temperature changes, and liquid interactions into corresponding mechanical, thermal or fluid damage. OOPSIEVERSE comprises two core elements: (1) DAMAGESIM, a simulator-agnostic framework for detecting and quantifying damage during navigation and manipulation, and (2) a suite of household tasks designed to evaluate common damage modes and distinguish between task completion and safe execution. We demonstrate the generality of our framework by instantiating DAMAGESIM in two simulators with different physics backends, OmniGibson (Nvidia Omniverse) and RoboCasa (MuJoCo). We further showcase the utility of OOPSIEVERSE across multiple use cases, including (1) guiding safer demonstration collection via real-time damage feedback, (2) learning safer manipulation policies through damage-conditioned imitation learning and reinforcement learning, (3) benchmarking the safety of state-of-the-art Vision Language Action policies, and (4) improving real-world safety of sim-to-real transferred policies. Together, our results highlight the potential of OOPSIEVERSE as an open-source foundation for systematic, scalable research on safe robot manipulation. For code and more information, please refer to https://robin-lab.cs.utexas.edu/oopsieverse/

</details>


### [202] [Human-as-Humanoid: Enabling Zero-Shot Humanoid Learning from Ego-Exo Human Videos with Human-Aligned Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32009)
*Xiaopeng Lin,Ruoqi Yang,Shijie Lian,Zhaolong Shen,Bin Yu,Changti Wu,Haibao Liu,Yuxiang Zhang,Hong Li,Qiyuan Su,Haochen Liu,Xuguo He,Yukun Shi,Cong Huang,Zhirui Zhang,Bojun Cheng,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Human-as-Humanoid框架，利用人类示教视频（ego-exo视角）通过运动恢复、逆运动学重定向和正运动学感知监督，将人类动作转换为高自由度人形机器人（PrimeU，60-DoF）可执行的观察-动作监督信号，实现无需机器人遥操作即可训练VLA策略。实验证明数据采集效率提升4.8-7.2倍，下游任务零机器人示教即可泛化到真实部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人VLA模型训练需要高质量的观察-动作监督数据，但遥操作采集数据成本高、扩展难；而人类日常视频虽然包含丰富的双臂操作，却无法直接提供可执行的机器人动作。因此需要一种框架将人类示教转化为机器人可用的监督信号。

Method: 1）使用PrimeU（60-DoF上半身人形机器人，与人类体态对齐）；2）同步ego-exo视频：ego视角提供部署对齐的观察，exo视角用于运动恢复；3）通过分阶段逆运动学（IK）将恢复的人体运动重定向为控制器对齐的60-DoF动作块；4）采用正运动学（FK）感知的监督训练方式，保留腕部和指尖的任务空间几何信息。

Result: 1）数据采集效率比传统人形遥操作提升4.8-7.2倍；2）仅用转换后的人类标签进行后训练的策略，无需目标任务的机器人示教即可泛化到真实机器人部署；3）在运动恢复、机器人动作空间和真实部署三个层面验证了转换链条的有效性。

Conclusion: Human-as-Humanoid框架成功将大规模人类视觉示教转化为高自由度人形机器人可用的动作监督，显著降低了人形机器人数据采集门槛，为VLA模型训练提供了可扩展的数据解决方案，有望推动人形机器人从遥操作依赖向人类数据驱动范式的转变。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models across robot embodiments require high-quality observation--action supervision to learn deployable action distributions, yet scaling such robot data remains difficult, especially for high-DoF humanoids. Teleoperation provides controller-aligned supervision, while human egocentric videos capture diverse bimanual manipulation but do not directly provide executable robot actions. We introduce Human-as-Humanoid, a human-to-humanoid supervision framework that enables near-real-time human-centric action generation, making human demonstrations usable for high-DoF humanoid VLA training by jointly aligning the robot embodiment, the sensing setup, and the action-label interface. Built on PrimeU, a human-aligned 60-DoF upper-body humanoid, Human-as-Humanoid uses synchronized ego-exo videos to pair deployment-aligned egocentric observations with exocentric motion recovery, retargets the recovered human motion through staged Inverse Kinematics (IK) into controller-aligned 60-DoF action chunks, and trains the VLA model with Forward Kinematics (FK)-aware supervision to preserve wrist and fingertip task-space geometry. This converts large-scale human demonstrations from visual observations into executable observation--action supervision for the target humanoid. Experiments validate the conversion chain at the motion-recovery, robot-action-space, and real-robot deployment levels. Human-as-Humanoid yields a 4.8--7.2x raw demonstration-throughput gain over humanoid teleoperation in our data-collection analysis, and on several downstream tasks, policies post-trained only with the converted human labels generalize to real-robot deployment without target-task robot demonstrations. The official project website is available at https://zgc-embodyai.github.io/Human-as-Humanoid.

</details>


### [203] [Freeform Preference Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32027)
*Marcel Torne,Anubha Mahajan,Abhijnya Bhat,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FPL是一种从自由形式人类偏好中学习机器人策略的方法，允许标注者用自然语言定义偏好轴并提供成对偏好，从而训练语言条件奖励模型和奖励条件策略，在长时程操作任务中比稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法提升38个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励设计方法存在瓶颈：稀疏成功标签信号过弱，二元偏好将多个竞争性质量概念压缩为单一模糊信号，难以有效指导机器人在长时程操作任务中的策略改进。

Method: 让标注者用自然语言定义偏好轴（如速度、安全性、放置质量等），并沿每个轴提供成对偏好；利用这些标注学习语言条件奖励模型，将轨迹和偏好标签映射为轴特定奖励；再训练奖励条件策略以优化多个维度的表现。

Result: 在四个真实世界和两个模拟长时程操作任务中，FPL相比稀疏奖励和二元偏好方法提升了38个百分点；学习到密集的进度信号，无需显式子任务分割；展现出数据中未见的组合行为；允许用户在测试时调整策略行为而无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 自由形式偏好学习能够更丰富地捕捉人类对不同行为维度的偏好，从而有效提升机器人长时程操作策略的性能和可控性。

Abstract: Reward design remains a central bottleneck for autonomous robot policy improvement, especially in long-horizon manipulation tasks where sparse success labels provide too little signal and binary preferences collapse many competing notions of quality into one ambiguous signal. We introduce Freeform Preference Learning (FPL), a method for learning robot policies from freeform human preferences. Rather than asking annotators which of two trajectories is better overall, FPL lets them define natural-language preference axes, such as speed, safety, quality of placement, or carefulness, and provide pairwise preferences along each axis. These annotations are used to learn a language-conditioned reward model that maps a trajectory and preference label to an axis-specific reward. We use this model to train a reward-conditioned policy that optimizes across the multiple human-specified dimensions. Across four real-world and two simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, FPL improves over sparse-reward and binary-preference methods by 38 percentage points. Beyond improved performance, FPL learns dense progress signals without explicit subtask segmentation, shows compositionality of behavior not present in the data, and allows users to steer the policy towards different behaviors at test time without retraining. Blog post with videos available at https://freeform-pl.github.io/fpl.website/

</details>


### [204] [DVG-WM: Disentangled Video Generation Enables Efficient Embodied World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.32028)
*Ziyu Shan,Zhenyu Wu,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DVG-WM框架，将视频基础的世界模型解耦为动力学学习与视觉合成两部分，通过级联机制实现高效机器人操作预测，速度提升达3.97倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频基础的世界模型将动力学建模与高分辨率视觉合成纠缠在一起，导致要么推理速度慢难以用于迭代规划，要么预测过于粗糙丢失接触细节。

Method: 1) 显式解耦：将世界模型分解为动力学学习（预测中间视觉状态序列）和视觉合成（生成高保真视频）；2) 级联机制：用流匹配将动力学映射到视频潜在空间，引入潜在退化机制再生接触细节。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实平台上，视频质量提升，推理速度最高加速3.97倍，验证了解耦视频生成可作为高效的具身世界模型。

Conclusion: 解耦视频生成策略有效解决了精度与速度的矛盾，为机器人操作中的世界建模提供了高效可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Video-based embodied world models provide an appealing substrate for robotic manipulation by predicting future states, yet current approaches remain limited by a fundamental entanglement: accurately modeling dynamics typically requires low-level temporal reasoning, while producing high-resolution frames demands expansive visual synthesis according to high-level semantics. This entanglement results in slow inference speed for iterative planning or too coarse predictions to retain contact-rich details. To solve this dilemma, we present Disentangled Video Generation World Model (DVG-WM), an efficient framework that explicitly decomposes world modeling into dynamics learning and visual synthesis. Conditioned on an initial observation and a language instruction, our model first generates a plausible sequence of intermediate visual states to preview the physical interaction and refines them to obtain high-fidelity videos. Furthermore, an efficient cascading mechanism is proposed, where DVG-WM uses flow matching to directly map the dynamics to video latents, and introduces a latent degradation mechanism to regenerate contact-rich details. Experiments on LIBERO and real-world platforms demonstrate improved video quality with up to 3.97 times acceleration, validating that disentangled video generation can be an efficient embodied world model for robotic manipulation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [What Drives Interactive Improvement from Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30774)
*Bartłomiej Cupiał,Jan Łojek,Mikołaj Garstecki,Szymon Pobłocki,Alicja Ziarko,Piotr Miłoś*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控师生实验协议，系统研究了多轮语言智能体中自然语言反馈的真实效用。研究发现：自我反馈效果与盲目重试相当，只有外部强教师才能带来显著增益；交互提升主要依赖学生利用反馈的能力，而非教师身份。作者呼吁应使用重试基线来评估反馈型智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮对话语言智能体中，最终准确率的提升可能源自反馈，也可能来自重采样、格式修正或额外测试时计算。现有研究往往将多轮改进直接归因于反馈，缺乏对混杂因素的剥离。作者希望区分"真正的反馈驱动提升"与"重试带来的伪增益"。

Method: 设计受控的师生实验协议，在Omni-MATH、Codeforces、BBEH Linguini、ARC-AGI1四个基准上，用13个开源模型分别扮演学生和教师。比较外部反馈、自我反馈和无引导自我修正三种设置，同时控制交互历史、任务难度、教师是否获得特权信息等变量，构建密集的师生交互矩阵。

Result: 1）多轮改进本身并非反馈有效性的证据；2）自我生成的反馈相比无引导自我修正几乎没有额外收益；3）最强外部教师能带来显著的反馈特异性增益；4）交互提升更多由学生利用反馈的能力驱动，而非教师身份；5）教师选择在固定学生下仍很重要。

Conclusion: 评估基于反馈的智能体时应与重试基线对照；能否有效利用反馈（而非反馈的可得性）是交互式提升的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: We study when natural-language feedback produces improvement beyond the gains obtainable from repeated attempts alone. In multi-turn language agent setting, higher final accuracy can reflect useful feedback, but it can also arise from resampling, format correction, or additional test-time computation. To separate these effects, we introduce a controlled student-teacher protocol across Omni-MATH, Codeforces, BBEH Linguini, and ARC-AGI1, evaluating thirteen open-weight models in both student and teacher roles. We compare external feedback, self-feedback, and unguided self-refinement, while varying interaction history, task difficulty, and teacher access to privileged task information. Across settings, we find that multi-turn improvement is often not evidence of feedback use: self-generated feedback adds little beyond unguided self-refinement, whereas the strongest external teachers produce substantially larger feedback-specific gains, suggesting that useful feedback must provide guidance beyond generic retry. Dense student-teacher interaction matrices further show that interactive gains are driven more by the student's ability to use feedback than by the teacher's identity, although teacher choice remains important for a fixed student. These results suggest that feedback-based agents should be evaluated against repeated-attempt baselines, and that ability to act on feedback, not merely feedback availability, is a central bottleneck for interactive improvement. We release our controlled student-teacher evaluation framework at https://j-lojek.github.io/feedback-generation-is-a-bottleneck/.

</details>


### [206] [Contrastive Reflection for Iterative Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30840)
*Derek Koh,Jinghui Mo,Benjamin H. Le,Jiening Zhan,Baofen Zheng,Kevin Bevis,Nathaniel C. Owen,Lauren Elizabeth Charney,Wenqiong Liu,Jingwei Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了对比反思（Contrastive Reflection）框架，用于迭代优化IR智能体的提示词。该框架通过识别错误锚定的行为切片、添加附近成功示例，让教师LLM提出针对性修改，并在验证集性能提升后才接受修改。在HotpotQA上，一次修复将准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%，优于仅失败样本或随机样本的变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进IR智能体的提示词本质上是一个优化问题，但在实际应用中更像是调试过程：工程师需要知道哪个行为失败了、哪些相近行为仍然有效、两者的区别是什么、以及修改是否提升了保留集质量且没有引入回归。现有的方法缺乏这种结构化的可解释调试手段。

Method: 对比反思框架：1) 从任务中心的质量定义出发，QA智能体暴露出检索/推理轨迹，评分智能体暴露出维度级分数和理由；2) 用这些结构化轨迹识别错误锚定的行为切片；3) 从同一区域添加附近成功示例；4) 让教师LLM提出有针对性的提示词编辑；5) 仅在验证性能提升时接受候选编辑，可选进行回归检查。采用基于树的切片选择器实例化该框架。

Result: 在HotpotQA上，一次树选择的对比修复将保留集精确匹配准确率从51.4%提升至60.4%。仅失败样本和随机样本的变体改进更少且破坏了更多先前正确的示例。与MIPROv2（59.4%）和GEPA（57.0%）对比接近或有优势。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可解释的、验证驱动的优化循环，使IR智能体的提示修复更具可检查性。核心贡献是对比反思循环本身，而非具体的树选择器实现。

Abstract: LLM agents are becoming central to information retrieval: they issue retrieval queries, synthesize answers, and increasingly serve as judges for IR evaluation. Improving the prompts that control these agents is an optimization problem, but in applied IR settings it often looks less like blind search and more like debugging. Engineers need to know which behavior failed, which nearby behavior still worked, what distinguishes the two, and whether a prompt edit improves held-out quality without introducing regressions.
  We present Contrastive Reflection, an iterative prompt-optimization framework for agentic IR workflows. The framework starts from a task-centric quality definition: QA agents expose retrieval or reasoning traces, and grading agents expose dimension-level scores and rationales. These structured traces are used to identify error-anchored behavioral slices, add nearby successful examples from the same region, and ask a Teacher LLM to propose a targeted prompt edit. Candidate edits are accepted only when validation performance improves, optionally subject to regression checks. We instantiate the framework with a tree-based slice selector, but the contribution is the contrastive reflection loop rather than the tree itself.
  On a public HotpotQA retrieval-augmented QA setup, one tree-selected contrastive repair improves held-out exact-match accuracy from 51.4% to 60.4%. Failure-only and random-evidence variants improve less and break more previously correct examples. A light instruction-only comparison places the method near modern prompt optimizers: MIPROv2 reaches 59.4% and GEPA 57.0%. The result is an interpretable optimization loop for IR agents, aimed at making prompt repair more inspectable and validation-driven.

</details>


### [207] [How Can AI Find My Model? A Model-Finding Experimental Study Considering Data Formats, Embeddings, and Retrieval Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30846)
*Jhon G. Botello,Jose J. Padilla,Erika Frydenlund,Krzysztof Rechowicz,Eric Weisel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了数据表示、嵌入模型和检索策略对基于自然语言查询的仿真模型发现的影响，发现数据表示很重要、开源嵌入模型表现良好、重排序方法在复杂查询下尤为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真模型复用面临挑战，当大量模型共存时，如何识别与建模意图相符的模型仍很困难，亟需在语义层面实现高效的模型发现。

Method: 采用实验研究方法，系统评估了不同数据表示、基于Transformer的嵌入模型以及检索策略对仿真模型发现性能的影响，使用recall@5和nDCG@5等标准信息检索指标对多种查询类型进行评测。

Result: 实验结果表明：数据表示方式对检索效果有显著影响；开源嵌入模型能达到较高性能；重排序方法在查询复杂度增加时尤为重要。

Conclusion: 该工作为AI驱动的模型发现提供了基线，并对推动AI驱动的可组合性和互操作性具有参考意义。

Abstract: Discovering simulation models for reuse remains a fundamental challenge in Modeling and Simulation (M&S). When many models coexist, identifying those that align with a given modeling intent remains difficult. Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI), particularly retrieval-based approaches, offer a promising pathway to operate at this semantic layer. In this paper, we present an experimental study investigating the impact of data representation, transformer-based embedding models, and retrieval strategies on the discovery of simulation models using natural language queries. We evaluated performance across multiple query types using standard information retrieval metrics, including recall@5 and nDCG@5. Results show that data representation matters, open-source embedding models can achieve high performance, and reranking methods are important, especially as query complexity increases. This work provides a baseline for AI-driven model discovery and discusses its role in advancing toward AI-driven composability and interoperability.

</details>


### [208] [BayesBench: Evaluating LLM Belief Trajectories Under Multi-Turn Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30850)
*Ankur Samanta,Akshayaa Magesh,Tal Lancewicki,Ayush Jain,Youliang Yu,Paul Sajda,Kaveh Hassani,Aditya Modi,Daniel R. Jiang,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BayesBench基准，评估LLM在多轮对话中是否像贝叶斯理性推理者那样更新信念。实验发现：模型规模越大，潜变量推理能力越强，但将推理结果转化为下游预测时存在明显断裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测只关注单轮答案的正确性，忽略了多轮对话中模型应基于新证据持续更新信念的过程。作者质疑LLM的信念更新是否接近理性贝叶斯推理者。

Method: 设计BayesBench，包含三个递进任务：贝叶斯估计（推断未知参数）、贝叶斯预测（将潜变量信念转化为预测结果）、潜变量框架贝叶斯预测（联合推理潜状态和用户画像）。在7个LLM（3B-70B）上测试。

Result: 模型规模扩大会提升潜变量推理和证据累积能力，有时更新接近贝叶斯后验；但这些提升不能可靠地迁移到下游预测任务上。

Conclusion: LLM在推断潜变量和利用其进行理性信念更新之间存在能力断层，即“推理-预测鸿沟”。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically deployed in multi-turn conversations, where each turn provides new evidence that should reduce epistemic uncertainty about their environment. Acting rationally then requires inferring the unobserved quantities that govern it and updating beliefs about them as evidence accumulates. Yet most evaluations only score the model's final-turn answer in a single-turn format, leaving this process unexamined. We ask how closely LLMs' belief updates match those of a rational Bayesian reasoner in multi-turn settings, and introduce BayesBench, a suite of simulation environments that probe this across three progressively complex tasks: (i) Bayesian estimation, where the model infers an unknown parameter from sequential evidence; (ii) Bayesian prediction, where the model turns inferred beliefs about a latent variable into outcome forecasts; and (iii) latent-framed Bayesian prediction, where observations are filtered through a user-persona framing, requiring joint inference over the latent state and the persona. Across seven LLMs (3B--70B), scaling improves latent inference and evidence accumulation, with updates occasionally matching the Bayesian posterior. However, these gains do not reliably carry over to downstream prediction, exposing a gap between inferring latent structure and using it to rationally update beliefs about the target outcome.

</details>


### [209] [When Does Learning to Stop Help? A Cost-Aware Study of Early Exits in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30852)
*Zhe Dong,Fang Qin,Manish Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究推理语言模型的停止规则，提出LearnStop方法，通过从推理前缀中提取在线特征（如置信度、熵、投票份额、答案稳定性等）来预测前缀是否正确。实验发现，学习式停止在自由格式数学任务上有显著提升（GSM8K上自适应增益+0.157），但在多项选择和极难问题上，简单的标量规则（置信度、熵、稳定性）更具竞争力。结论是学习式停止并非通用替代方案，其价值取决于轨迹结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理模型在不同实例上消耗的计算量差异很大，但尚不清楚何时学习式停止规则能优于简单的置信度或收敛阈值。作者希望系统性地回答这一问题。

Method: 提出LearnStop方法：在固定预算检查点，从推理前缀中提取在线特征（答案置信度、熵、前缀投票份额、答案稳定性、回溯标记密度等），预测前缀正确性，从而决定是否停止推理。

Result: 在18个任务-模型设置上（GSM8K、MATH-500、MMLU-Pro、AIME-90、GPQA等），学习式停止在自由格式数学上改进固定预算前沿，GSM8K+Qwen3-32B上自适应增益+0.157，验证选择操作点保持正增益，比最强标量基线+0.028。但在多项选择和极难问题上，标量规则更具竞争力。

Conclusion: 学习式停止并非通用替代方案，它的有用性取决于任务轨迹结构：当许多问题在完全预算之前变正确但没有单一可靠标量信号时，学习式停止有用；当置信度或答案收敛已解决问题时，其收益基本消失。

Abstract: Reasoning models spend different amounts of useful computation across instances, but it remains unclear when a learned stopping rule improves over simple confidence or convergence thresholds. We study this question with LearnStop, a hidden-state-free checkpoint stopper for reasoning language models. At fixed budget checkpoints, LearnStop probes a short answer from the current reasoning prefix and predicts prefix correctness from online features such as answer confidence, entropy, prefix vote share, answer stability, and backtracking-marker density. Across 18 task-model settings spanning GSM8K, MATH-500, MMLU-Pro, AIME-90, GPQA, Qwen3, and DeepSeek-R1 distillations, the answer is task-dependent. On free-form math, learned multi-feature stopping improves the fixed-budget frontier and often beats scalar exits: on GSM8K with Qwen3-32B, the empirical frontier reaches a post-hoc peak adapt gain of +0.157, validation-selected operating points preserve positive gains, and the paired gain over the strongest scalar baseline is +0.028. On multiple-choice and very hard settings, scalar confidence, entropy, or stability rules are competitive or stronger. We therefore frame learned stopping not as a universal replacement for scalar exits, but as a tool whose value depends on trajectory structure. We further provide validation-selected operating points, paired bootstrap tests, finite-grid lost-correct risk calibration, cost accounting under KV-fork, prefix-cache, and black-box regimes, H100 serving profiles, checkpoint-schedule sweeps, transfer analyses, and robustness checks. The main practical finding is that learned stopping is useful when many questions become correct before full budget but do not exhibit a single reliable scalar stopping signal; its benefits largely disappear when confidence or answer convergence already solves the stopping problem.

</details>


### [210] [Beyond expert users: agents should help users construct preferences, not just elicit them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30863)
*Irena Saracay,Ludwig Schmidt,Carlos Guestrin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出传统AI代理假设用户有完整偏好是不现实的，并引入CoPref模型（基于信息经济学框架）来描述用户如何在对话中构建偏好。通过CoShop基准测试评估5个前沿模型，发现代理准确率不超过56%，主要问题不在于找物品，而在于未能帮助用户扩展对自身需求的认识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代理假设用户拥有完整且明确的偏好，但现实情况是用户往往缺乏领域知识，无法形成完备偏好。这导致代理在任务未明确指定时默认追问，但用户可能无法回答这些追问。

Method: 基于信息经济学的Search-Experience-Credence框架，提出CoPref模型来形式化用户如何通过代理对话构建偏好。并构建CoShop交互式基准测试来具体研究这一概念。

Result: 评估5个前沿模型，在5轮交互下准确率均不超过56%。

Conclusion: 代理失败的主要原因不是找不到物品，而是交互过程未能有效扩展用户对自身需求的认识。

Abstract: Agents typically assume an expert user -- one with well-formed preferences about what they want -- and default to clarifying questions whenever the task is underspecified. We argue this assumption is unrealistic. Users often lack the domain knowledge to have completely specified preferences; if asked about their preference on some feature, the user may be unable to answer without the agent helping the user to learn some domain knowledge needed to form a preference for that feature, e.g., via examples or explanations. To formalize these principles, we draw on the Search-Experience-Credence framework from Information Economics to introduce CoPref, a model of how users construct preferences based on agent dialog actions. We then study these ideas concretely in agentic recommender systems, proposing CoShop, an interactive benchmark. In CoShop, an agent converses with and makes recommendations for a CoPref user. The agent's performance depends on whether it can help the user gain the knowledge needed to specify the task well. Evaluating five frontier models, we find that no agent exceeds 56% accuracy on CoShop despite five turns of interaction. Failures stem not from agents' ability to find items, but from how little the interaction expands what users know about what they want.

</details>


### [211] [Investigating Multi-Agent Deliberation in Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30906)
*Cor Steging,Ludi van Leeuwen,Tadeusz Zbiegień*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索了基于大语言模型的多智能体协商方法在法律推理任务中的应用，提出了两种受法庭程序和法律论证启发的新型多智能体框架。实验表明，多智能体框架在整体性能上与基线大语言模型相当，但能产生显著不同的答案，尤其在需要多角度批判性思维的问题上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域中基于大语言模型的自主AI智能体（尤其是多智能体方法）尚缺乏系统探索，本文旨在填补这一空白，研究多智能体协商方法在法律推理任务中的潜力。

Method: 提出了两种受法庭程序和法律论证启发的新型多智能体协商框架，并在法律和非法律基准数据集上进行实验，比较多智能体框架与基线大语言模型的性能。

Result: 多智能体框架与基线模型整体性能相当，但产生显著不同的答案；能解决基线模型无法处理的案例，反之亦然；在需要多角度批判性思维的问题上表现更优。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统是AI在法律领域的一个有前景的方向，受法律启发的多智能体协商方法展示了独特的优势，尤其是在需要多视角思考的复杂任务上。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence is increasingly applied to the field of law, and has the potential to increase access to justice. One particular movement that is gaining traction is that of agentic AI, wherein AI agents, based on Large Language Models (LLMs) can take autonomous actions. In particular, multi-agent approaches in the legal domain remain largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate multi-agent deliberation methods for legal reasoning tasks using LLMs. We explore multi-agent deliberation (MAD) and introduce two novel multi-agent frameworks inspired by courtroom procedures and legal argumentation. Our experiments on both legal and non-legal benchmarks reveal that multi-agent frameworks achieve comparable overall performance to baseline large language models, but produce significantly distinct answers. Notably, these approaches can successfully solve cases that the baseline fails to address, and vice versa. We conduct a qualitative evaluation and highlight scenarios where multi-agent frameworks outperform monolithic approaches. For example, multi-agent approaches appear better suited for answering questions that require critical thinking from multiple perspectives. Our work positions multi-agent systems as a promising direction for AI in the legal domain, while demonstrating the potential of law-inspired multi-agent approaches for deliberation.

</details>


### [212] [Why Solve It Twice? Hierarchical Accumulation of Skills for Transfer-Efficient ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30911)
*Yongbin Kim,Yashar Talebirad,Osmar R. Zaiane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HASTE是一个分层多智能体系统，通过三级知识组织（全局、领域、竞赛特定），实现跨竞赛知识复用，在22个Kaggle竞赛中达到77.3%的奖牌率，相比平铺加载减少了52%的优化迭代次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML工程智能体每次竞赛都从零开始，浪费算力重复发现已知技术，缺乏跨竞赛知识复用机制。

Method: 提出HASTE分层多智能体系统，包含三范围层级（全局、领域、竞赛特定）和匹配的智能体层级，由协调器管理领域专家并通过LLM驱动的抽象促进层级间学习。

Result: 分层加载在保持159项技能不变时达到100%奖牌率（平铺加载仅62.5%）；在MLE-Bench Lite基准上达77.3%奖牌率；热启动减少52%优化迭代；技能采纳率从42%升至85%。

Conclusion: 更好的知识组织可以部分替代模型强度和计算预算，为ML工程智能体提供更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: ML engineering agents waste compute rediscovering known techniques because every competition is a cold start. We present HASTE, a hierarchical multi-agent system that organizes cross-competition knowledge into three scope tiers (global, domain, and competition-specific), each coupled to a matching agent level. An orchestrator coordinates domain specialists and promotes learning between tiers via LLM-driven abstraction. A controlled ablation provides evidence for scoped loading: holding a 159-skill inventory constant across 8 competitions, tiered loading achieves a 100% medal rate while flat loading reaches only 62.5%, the same medal rate as loading no skills, and consumes 2x the output tokens. On the full MLE-Bench Lite benchmark (22 Kaggle competitions), HASTE reaches a medal rate of 77.3% using Claude Sonnet 4.6 at 12h per competition. In a cold-start run, the system begins with no accumulated skills. In warm-start runs, it reloads skills learned from earlier competitions, using only global and domain-level skills for transfer across competitions. Warm starts use 52% fewer refinement iterations, and the fraction of proposed changes kept by the agent rises from 42% at low inventory to 85% once 50+ skills are available. These results suggest that better knowledge organization can partly substitute for model strength and compute budget in ML-engineering agents.

</details>


### [213] [RoPoLL: Robust Panel of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30931)
*Anish Acharya,Kris W Pan,Brian Verkhovsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化分析了LLM陪审团（PoLL）在Huber污染模型下的统计缺陷，发现只要存在任意正向污染，PoLL就会产生无界偏差。作者提出RoPoLL，用几何中位数作为鲁棒聚合函数替代简单共识，在理论最优断裂点1/2下达到参数率误差界，并在13个开源评判模型、3个基准和4种腐败场景下显著优于PoLL，尤其在重尾拜占庭攻击下提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM陪审团（PoLL）在实际中被广泛使用，但其统计行为未被严格理解。作者发现PoLL在存在偏差污染（如模式崩溃、谄媚、安全拒绝）时，无论陪审团规模多大，都会产生无界偏差，亟需一种鲁棒的方法来替代简单的共识聚合。

Method: 将LLM陪审团形式化为Huber污染模型下的鲁棒均值估计问题，提出RoPoLL：保留PoLL的多个评判模型，但将聚合函数替换为几何中位数（GM），这是一种无需调参、具有最优有限样本断裂点1/2的鲁棒均值估计器。作者推导了有限样本误差界和匹配的信息论极小化下界，并分析了与不可处理的Tukey半空间中位数之间的统计-计算差距。

Result: 在13个开源评判模型（4B-675B）、3个奖励模型基准和4种腐败机制（最高50%污染率）上，RoPoLL在所有有偏腐败类型上均优于PoLL：交叉维度攻击下提升约19%，重尾拜占庭攻击下提升数个数量级。3个评判模型组成的RoPoLL委员会（38B）在HelpSteer-2基准上、30%双峰随机腐败下，以1.31倍的准确率超越Mistral-Large-3（675B），实现18倍的参数效率优势。

Conclusion: PoLL在有偏污染下存在根本性统计缺陷；通过鲁棒均值估计（几何中位数）替换简单聚合，RoPoLL在理论上和实践上都能有效抵抗各类有偏攻击，以更小的模型规模实现更高的评估准确率。噪声GT对照实验证实，RoPoLL的性能优势来自抵抗有偏污染，而非应对良性不精确性。

Abstract: The LLM Jury, a Panel of LLM Evaluators (PoLL) reporting consensus scores, has become a practical alternative to single-judge LLM evaluation, yet its statistical behavior remains poorly understood. We formalize the LLM Jury under the Huber contamination model and show that PoLL incurs unbounded bias
  under any positive contamination, regardless of jury size, whenever a single judge fails in a biased, LLM-typical way (mode collapse, sycophancy, safety refusal). Framing jury consensus as classical robust mean estimation, we propose RoPoLL (Robust Panel of LLM-as-Judge), which preserves the PoLL
  panel but replaces the aggregation function with a robust mean estimator, instantiated with the geometric median (GM): tuning-free, with the optimal finite-sample breakdown point 1/2. A finite-sample error bound and a matching information-theoretic minimax lower bound agree on the parametric rate
  sigma*sqrt(d/N) and differ on the breakdown floor by a factor of sqrt(d), a statistical-computational gap that polynomial-time RoPoLL pays relative to the intractable Tukey halfspace median. Across 13 open-weight judges (4B-675B), three reward-model benchmarks, and four corruption regimes at rates up
  to 50%, RoPoLL dominates PoLL on every biased corruption type: by about 19% on cross-dimensional attacks at matched compute, and by orders of magnitude on heavy-tailed Byzantine adversaries. A 3-judge RoPoLL committee at 38B beats Mistral-Large-3 (675B) by 1.31x on HelpSteer-2 under 30% bimodal-random
  corruption, an 18x parameter advantage at better accuracy; a Noisy-GT control confirms the premium is paid against biased contamination, not benign imprecision.

</details>


### [214] [AgRefactor: Self-Evolving Agentic Workflow for HLS Compatibility and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30949)
*Yang Zou,Zijian Ding,Yizhou Sun,Jason Cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgRefactor是一个基于LLM的多智能体工作流，用于将软件代码重构为HLS兼容程序，它结合了自进化记忆系统和自动化重构工具，在11个真实基准测试中9个达到或超越现有最优方法，并实现了6.51x的加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将真实世界软件转化为可综合HLS代码仍然困难，现有自动化方法和基于LLM的重构方法缺乏灵活性、难以扩展且计算成本高。

Method: 提出AgRefactor，一个LLM-based多智能体工作流，包含自进化记忆系统（累积和检索事实性与策略性知识），并集成自动化重构工具以平衡LLM驱动改写与高效工具转换。

Result: 在11个基准测试中9个超越或匹配SOTA方法；性能优化实现6.51x几何平均加速比（对比SoTA调优工具），1.20x加速比（对比优化开源设计），额外资源开销低于20%。

Conclusion: AgRefactor是一种全自动化、开源的高效HLS重构框架，在复杂真实基准上展现了优越的性能和扩展性。

Abstract: High-Level Synthesis (HLS) provides a fast path from concepts to silicon, but converting real-world software into synthesizable HLS code remains challenging due to restrictive language support and the gap between software and hardware programming practices. Existing automated and LLM-based refactoring approaches partially address this problem, yet they often lack flexibility, struggle to scale, and incur high computational costs. We introduce AgRefactor, an LLM-based multi-agent workflow for refactoring software into HLS-compatible programs. AgRefactor incorporates a self-evolving memory system that accumulates and retrieves factual and strategic knowledge across tasks, improving robustness and efficiency on unseen programs. To reduce cost and enhance scalability, it integrates automated refactoring tools, enabling agents to balance LLM-driven rewrites with efficient tool-based transformations. On 9 out of 11 challenging real-world benchmarks, which are 5-10x longer than the most complex cases studied in prior work, AgRefactor outperforms or matches the state-of-the-art automated refactoring tool and a strong LLM-based baseline built on the same framework backbone. Further agentic performance optimization yields a 6.51x geometric mean speedup over the SoTA pragma tuning tool and a 1.20x speedup over optimized open-source designs with less than 20% extra resources. AgRefactor is fully-automated and open-sourced.

</details>


### [215] [Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network: Explainable Deep Learning for Cybersecurity Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30953)
*Nicolaie Popescu-Bodorin,Madeleine Togher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经-贝叶斯-符号残差注意力浅层网络（NBS-RASN），用80个可解释神经元和12层结构实现可解释的网络安全风险评估，在20个开源项目上达到0.79-0.97置信度，证明浅层网络结合深层推理可超越黑盒深度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度模型在网络安全风险评估中为了准确性牺牲了可解释性，而在高风险的开源生态系统安全评估中，可解释性是至关重要的，因此需要一种既能保持准确性又具备内在可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出NBS-RASN混合架构：1）浅层网络（80个神经元，12层）；2）将领域知识、因果推理和专家判断编码为可微分组件；3）门控机制强制执行五项认知公理（精确性、因果性、可证伪性、透明性、完整性）作为硬约束；4）通过残差注意力和反馈循环模拟深度学习特性；5）输出完全可分解的分数：确定性加权组件+专家调整项，每项调整可追溯至命名放大器。

Result: 在涵盖所有OWASP Top 10:2025类别和语言风险等级的20个开源项目上验证，达到0.79-0.97的置信度分数。

Conclusion: 证明了可解释性是由设计保证的而非训练算法保证的，挑战了深度学习必须依赖深层网络的假设，表明具有深层推理能力的浅层网络在高风险网络安全场景中可以超越不透明模型。

Abstract: We introduce the Neuro-Bayesian-Symbolic Residual Attention Shallow Network (NBS-RASN), a hybrid neural architecture for explainable cybersecurity risk assessment in open-source ecosystems. Unlike deep models that trade interpretability for accuracy, our shallow network encodes domain knowledge, causal reasoning, and expert judgment as differentiable components. It uses 80 interpretable neurons across 12 layers, including a gatekeeper that enforces five epistemological axioms - precision, causality, falsifiability, transparency, and completeness - as hard constraints before propagation. Despite limited depth, the network exhibits deep-learning traits via residual attention and feedback loops, learning complex risk patterns without becoming a black box. It produces fully decomposable scores: a deterministic weighted component plus an expert adjustment, with each adjustment traceable to named amplifiers (blast radius, propagation speed, structural nature, default exposure, exploitation pattern, institutional criticality). We validate on 20 open-source projects covering all OWASP Top 10:2025 categories and language risk classes, achieving confidence scores of 0.79-0.97, and show that explainability is guaranteed by design, not by a training algorithm. This challenges the assumption that deep learning requires deep networks, proving that shallow networks with deep reasoning can outperform opaque models in high-stakes cybersecurity, where interpretability is essential.

</details>


### [216] [HyPOLE: Hyperproperty-Guided Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30966)
*Arshia Rafieioskouei,Tzu-Han Hsu,Matthew Lucas,Borzoo Bonakdarpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyPOLE框架，利用超属性（HyperLTL）指导多智能体强化学习，结合CTDE技术，在SMAC等基准测试中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规约在单智能体RL中效果显著，但在多智能体部分可观测场景下未被充分探索。作者希望利用超属性的表达能力来指导MARL学习过程。

Method: 提出HyPOLE框架，使用HyperLTL逻辑表达超属性约束，并集成CTDE（集中训练-分散执行）范式来合成去中心化策略。

Result: 在SMAC、MessySMAC和WildFire三个基准上进行了评估，HyPOLE相比基线方法表现出明显优势。

Conclusion: 超属性逻辑可以有效指导MARL训练，HyPOLE验证了形式化规约在多智能体部分可观测场景下的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Formal specification is a powerful tool to guide the learning process and provides significant advantages over reward shaping: (1) mathematical rigor; (2) expressiveness to specify objectives and constraints, and (3) the ability to define tactics to achieve objectives. However, these benefits remain largely unexplored in the context of Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL).
